# Gacha Voice Presets — full catalog

> **Generated by `tools/presets_ingest.py` from `presets.csv`** — do not edit by hand.
> Edit the CSV, then re-run: `python tools/presets_ingest.py`

## Validation summary

- **1000 lines** = 125 bases (signature=yes) + 875 variants, in **25 classes**
- warnings: 0 (clean)
- pitch vocabulary: High (295), Low (244), Mid (192), Neutral (89), Soft (78), Deep (77), Synth (25)
- pace vocabulary: Drift (175), Measure (127), Dash (113), Lilt (110), Tale (104), Pulse (99), Lounge (89), Tick (70), Burst (69), Pause (44)
- texture vocabulary: Husk (100), Crystal (96), Honey (93), Silver (89), Bell (84), Wisp (73), Resonant (72), Velvet (65), Reed (64), Sunbeam (60), Gravel (54), Chrome (25)
- rarity: 5 (125), 4 (612), 3 (263)
- gender: Feminine (536), Masculine (400), Androgynous (64)

## How to order any line

Each line maps 1:1 onto the **58-feature vocabulary** (`seeds/README.md`):

- **pitch column** → pitch / resonance / weight / brightness (Physical group)
- **pace column** → rate / rhythm / emphasis / pausing (Delivery group)
- **texture** (2nd word of variant names) → roughness / wetness / velvety / breathiness / metallic (Physical group)

Prompt template for chat (or a seed design brief):

> new character with a **{class}** voice — {pitch} pitch, {texture} texture, {pace} delivery: “{description}”

### 1. Sunstrike — *base* (rarity 5 · High · Dash · Feminine)

> Teenage girl. A human firecracker who treats every day like a festival and every stranger like a future best friend. The voice is a bright, airy high register, carried on rapid-fire, excitable delivery. Giggles mid-sentence and restarts with double the energy. Underneath the noise, there is warmth that sneaks up on you.
